- **arboreto-grn-inference**: GRN inference from expression via GRNBoost2 (gradient boosting) or GENIE3 (Random Forest). Load matrix, filter by TFs, infer TF-target-importance links, save network. Dask-parallelized to single-cell scale. Core SCENIC component. - **arboreto-grn-inference**: 当需要从基因表达矩阵推断转录因子-靶基因调控网络（GRN）时使用；用 GRNBoost2（梯度提升）或 GENIE3（随机森林）输出 TF-target-importance 三元组并保存为网络/邻接表（pySCENIC 第 1 步）；不适用于已有网络的下游 regulon 识别与活性打分（交给 pySCENIC）、单细胞预处理（用 scanpy）。触发词：GRN、基因调控网络、GRNBoost2、GENIE3、arboreto、SCENIC 邻接矩阵 ([findscripter/everything-skills](https://github.com/findscripter/everything-skills/tree/HEAD/09-verticals/arboreto-grn-inference) · ★11 · 2 copies)
- **arboreto-grn-inference**: Gene regulatory network inference from expression data using GRNBoost2 (gradient boosting) or GENIE3 (Random Forest).
